    GROUP PROFILE
    集團簡介



    Time Interconnect Technology Limited (the “Company”, together with             匯聚科技有限公司（「本公司」，連同其附屬公
    its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is a well-established supplier of customised    司統稱「本集團」）是一家信譽卓著的定制電線
    interconnect solutions with 30 years experience in the industry and            互連方案供應商 ，在行業擁有30年經驗 ，並於
    expanded the business scale continuously in 2020 and 2021 covering             二零二零年及二零二一年持續擴大業務規模 ，
    digital cable business and automotive wire harness business. The Group is      涵蓋數字電線業務及汽車配線業務 。本集團的
    headquartered in Hong Kong, and has manufacturing facilities in Shanghai,      總部設於香港 ，並於中華人民共和國（「中國」）
    Suzhou and Huizhou, the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”).                    上海 、蘇州及惠州擁有生產設施 。

    The Group currently manufactures and supplies, a wide variety of copper &      本集團目前製造及供應各種銅纜和光纜電線組
    optical fiber cable assemblies and digital cable products which are produced   件及數字電線產品 ，產品均根據個別客戶夥伴
    to the specifications and designs, of its individual customer partners.        的詳述規格及設計而生產 。產品已使用在包括
    Its products are used by a number of established PRC and international         電訊 、數據中心 、工業設備 、醫療設備 、汽
    customers in a variety of market sectors, including telecommunication,         車配線及數字電線在內的各種市場領域 ，均受
    data centre, industrial equipment, medical equipment, automotive wire          不少商譽優良的中國及國際客戶所採用 。
    harness and digital cables.

    Its business features on customised cable assemblies and cable products for    我們的業務特點為定制電線組件及電線產品 ，
    a variety of specifications which are used in a wide range of applications.    適用於各種應用中的各種規格 。本集團按合約
    The Group supplies cable products on a Contract Manufacturing Services         生產服務(CMS)基準供應電線產品 ，有關產品
    (CMS) basis, and its cable products are made-to-order, upon receipt of its     在收到客戶訂單後定制生產 。
    customers’ orders.

    VISION                                                                         願景
    To be a leading provider of electronic interconnect products, continually       成為電纜互連產品的領先供應商 ，持續公認為
    recognised as a “Preferred Supplier” capable of delivering world class         「優選供應商」，積極地提供世界一流的品質 、
    quality, products and services – EACH and EVERY TIME.                           產品和服務 。

    STRATEGY                                                                       策略
    To become a Tier-1 supplier of cable products and high performance             成為5G電訊 、數據通訊 、醫療設備 、運輸及
    interconnect products to the market leaders in the 5G telecommunication,       工業領域的電線產品和高性能互連產品的一級
    data communication, healthcare, transport and industrial sectors.              供應商及市場領導者 。

     BUSINESS OVERVIEW                                                                業務回顧
     During the Review Year, although vaccines have been available, vaccine           於回顧年度 ，儘管疫苗已經推出 ，但疫苗普及
     promotion has been slow and virus variants have appeared. The pandemic           進展緩慢 ，且病毒出現變種 。全球部分地區的
     has taken a turn for the worse in some parts of the world since April            疫情自二零二一年四月起惡化 ，同時其他地區
     2021. Meanwhile, a speedy vaccine rollout has helped bringing down               在迅速推出疫苗下有效減低感染個案 。各國經
     caseloads quickly in other regions. Economies are diverging even further,        濟體的疫苗普及速度和政策支持力度的不同 ，
     influenced by differences in the pace of vaccine rollout and policy support.     導致彼此出現進一步分化 。獲取疫苗的能力
     Vaccine access has emerged as the principal fault line along which the           已成為將全球復甦劃分成兩方陣營的主要分界
     global recovery splits into two blocs: those that can look forward to further    線：一方有望於今年較後時間進一步恢復正常
     normalization of activity later this year (almost all advanced economies)        活動（幾乎都是發達經濟體）；另一方則仍將面
     and those that will still face resurgent infections and rising COVID-19          臨感染病例再度激增及2019冠狀病毒病死亡數
     death tolls. However, smooth and durable recoveries are not assured even         字持續上升 。然而 ，由於病毒繼續變異 、疫情
     in places where infections are seemingly under control, since the virus          似乎仍會持續並影響經濟 ，故即使在感染看似
     continues to mutate, the epidemic seems to continue and impact the               受控的地區 ，亦無法保證能夠平穩及持久地實
     economy. On the other hand, a large number of containers are stranded            現復甦 。另一方面 ，由於大量集裝箱滯留在業
     in ports with inactive business, and there is a serious shortage of containers   務不活躍的港口 ，市場上的集裝箱出現嚴重短
     in the market, which has led to a surge in transportation costs and freight      缺 ，導致運輸成本飆升及貨運延誤 ，並直接阻
     delays. The Group’s shipments have been obstructed directly and the              礙本集團出貨 ，抑制業務增長 。

     FOREIGN EXCHANGE EXPOSURE                                                       外匯風險
     Most of the Group’s receipts and payments are denominated in US dollars,        本集團大部分收付款以美元 、港元 、人民幣及
     Hong Kong dollars, RMB and Euro. The Group’s management monitors                歐元計值 。本集團管理層透過訂立遠期外匯合
     the risk of related foreign exchange exposure by entering into forward          約監察其相關外匯風險 。匯率波動可能對本集
     foreign exchange contracts. Foreign currency exchange rates are volatile        團業績產生影響 。本集團管理層持續評估本集
     and may have an impact on the Group’s results. The Group’s management           團的外幣風險並於必要時採取措施盡量降低本
     evaluates the Group’s foreign currency exposure on a continuing basis and       集團的風險 。
     takes actions to minimise the Group’s exposure whenever necessary.

     exchange exposure and will consider hedging significant foreign currency        將於需要時考慮對沖重大外幣風險 。除上市所
     exposure should the need arise. Save for the net proceeds from the listing,     得款項淨額外 ，本集團亦將監察及維持港元現
     the Group will also monitor and maintain a Hong Kong dollar cash balance        金結餘 ，以盡量減少對可能引致匯兌虧損的不
     in order to minimise the need for unnecessary foreign exchange conversion       必要外匯兌換的需求 。

DIRECTOR’S REPORT
董事會報告



The Board is pleased to present the annual report together with the audited                  董事會欣然提呈截至二零二二年三月三十一日止年
consolidated financial statements of the Group for the year ended 31 March 2022.             度的年報連同本集團的經審核綜合財務報表 。

PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                                         主要業務
The Company is an investment holding company, and its subsidiaries are principally           本公司為一間投資控股公司 ，而其附屬公司主要從
engaged in manufacture and sales of cable assembly products (the “Core                       事製造及銷售電線組件產品（「核心業務」）。其附屬公

   board diversity in terms of, among other things, gender, age, experience,                 事會的多樣性 ，其中包括性別 、年齡 、經驗 、

   objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of diversity on the Board.         選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事會多樣性的裨益 。

   cultural and educational background, expertise, skills and know-how.                      背景 、專業經驗 、技能及知識 。

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                                   風險管理及內部監控
The Directors acknowledge that they have overall responsibility for overseeing the     董事明白彼等有全盤責任監督本公司內部監控 、財
Company’s internal control, financial control and risk management system and shall     務監控及風險管理系統 ，並須持續監察其有效性 。
monitor its effectiveness on an ongoing basis. A review of the effectiveness of the    董事會至少每年對風險管理及內部監控系統的有效
risk management and internal control systems has been conducted by the Board at        性進行審閱 。
least annually.

Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material errors, losses or fraud,      本公司著力提供合理保證 ，避免出現重大錯誤 、損
the Company has established a risk management procedures which comprised the           失或造假 ，為此已成立風險管理程序 ，包括以下步
following steps:                                                                       驟：

–   Identify risks: Identify major and significant risks that could affect the         –   識別風險：識別可能影響達成本集團目標之主要
    achievement of goals of the Group;                                                     及重大風險；

–   Risk assessment: Assess and evaluate the identified risk according to its likely   –   風險評估：根據已識別風險的預料影響及出現之
    impact and the likelihood of occurrence;                                               可能性作出評估及評核；

–   Risk mitigation: Develop effective control activities to mitigate the risks.       –   紓緩風險：策劃有效的監管活動 ，務求紓緩風
                                                                                           險。

Risk identification and assessment is performed or updated annually, and the results   每年進行或更新風險識別及評估 ，風險評估 、評核
of risk assessment, evaluation and mitigation of each functions or operation are       的結果及各功能或營運之紓緩措施會詳細記錄在風
documented in the Risk Registry to communicate to the Board and Management             險資料冊內 ，以供董事會及管理層審閱 。
for reviews.

The Group’s risk management and internal control systems are, however, designed        然而 ，本集團之風險管理及內部監控系統之設計旨
to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives,    在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標之風險 ，僅可就
and can only provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material            重大失實聲明或損失提供合理而非絕對保證 。
misstatement or loss.

A review on the internal control systems of the Company, including financial,          獨立顧問公司內具有相關專業知識進行獨立審閱的
operational and compliance controls and risk management functions has been             員工 ，對本公司的內部監控系統（包括財務 、營運及
carried out by an independent consultancy company with staff in possession of          合規監控及風險管理職能）進行審閱 。
relevant expertise to conduct an independent review.

The Audit Committee reviewed the internal control review report issued by the          審核委員會已審閱由獨立顧問公司發佈的內部監控
independent consultancy company and the Company’s risk management and                  審閱報告 ，以及本公司截至二零二二年三月三十一
internal control systems in respect of the year ended 31 March 2022 and considered     日止年度的風險管理和內部監控系統 ，並認為其屬
that they are effective and adequate. The Board assessed the effectiveness of          有效及充份 。董事會透過考慮內部監控審閱報告及
internal control systems by considering the internal control review report and         審核委員會所進行的審閱（並同意有關審閱），評估內
reviews performed by the Audit Committee and concurred the same.                       部監控系統的有效性 。

Under Code Provision D.2.5, the Group should have an internal audit function.          根據企管守則條文D.2.5 ，本集團須設有內部審核
The Company has no internal audit function because the Company has maintained          職能 。本公司並無內部審核職能 ，因為本公司設有

Noise Pollution                                                                                            噪音污染
The Group engaged licensed third party to perform regular inspection at the                                本集團委聘持牌的第三方對噪音水平進行定
noise level to check whether the Group has complied with Measurement                                       期 檢 測 ， 以 檢 查 本 集 團 是 否 遵 守《工 作 場 所
of Physical Agents in Workplace Part 8: Noise (GBZ/T 189.8-2007). Various                                  物理因素測量第8部分：噪聲》          （GBZ/T 189.8-
checking points have been set at different parts in the PRC production                                     2007）
                                                                                                               。本集團於中國的生產設施內設立多個檢
facilities. No material non-compliance has been found.                                                     測點 ，並無發現任何重大違規事宜 。

Even though the PRC production facilities recorded no significant impact                                   即使中國的生產設施並未對鄰近社區造成重大
on noise pollution to the surrounding community, the Group always                                          噪音污染影響 ，本集團也一直努力就有可能的
commits to maintaining communication with the community nearby about                                       噪音問題與附近社區保持溝通 。本集團向工人
the potential noise issue. The Group provides appropriate protective gears                                 提供合適的防護裝備（例如耳塞等），以保護工
(such as earplug and etc.) to the workers in order to protect their hearing.                               人的聽力 。

OPINION                                                                                 意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Time Interconnect              本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計載列於第85至
Technology Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to       159頁的匯聚科技有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公
as the “Group”) set out on pages 85 to 159, which comprise the consolidated             司（統稱為「貴集團」）的綜合財務報表 ，其中包括於
statement of financial position as at 31 March 2022, and the consolidated statement     二零二二年三月三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至
of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, consolidated statement of             該日止年度的綜合損益及其他全面收益表 、綜合權
changes in equity and consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then            益變動表和綜合現金流量表 ，以及綜合財務報表附
ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including a summary of       註 ，包括主要會計政策概要 。
significant accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view of       我 們 認 為 ， 綜 合 財 務 報 表 已 根 據 香 港 會計師公會
the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31 March 2022, and of its        （「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的《香港財務報告準則》           （「《香
consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows for the year then     港財務報告準則》」）真實而中肯地反映了 貴集團於
ended in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”)              二零二二年三月三十一日的綜合財務狀況及截至該
issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and         日止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量 ，並已遵
have been properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure requirements of the        照香港《公司條例》的披露規定妥為擬備 。
Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                                       意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing                我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港審計準則》
(“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under those standards are          （「《香港審計準則》」）進行審計 。我們在該等準則下

accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (the            公會頒佈的《專業會計師道德守則》 （「守則」），我們獨
“Code”), and we have fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with     立於 貴集團 ，並已履行守則中的其他道德責任 。
the Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and          我們相信 ，我們所獲得的審計憑證能充足及適當地
appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.                                          為我們的審計意見提供基礎 。

KEY AUDIT MATTERS                                                                       關鍵審計事項

and we do not provide a separate opinion on these matters.

     Valuation of right-of-use assets and property, plant and equipment measured at revaluation model
     按重估模式估值使用權資產及物業 、廠房及設備

     We identified the valuation of right-of-use assets and property, plant   Our procedures in relation to the management’s valuation of the
     and equipment at revaluation model as a key audit matter due to          Land and Buildings at revaluation model included:
     the inherent level of subjective judgements and complex estimates
     required in determining the revalued values.                             •   Evaluating the competence, capability, and objectivity of the
                                                                                  Valuer;
     The Group’s leasehold land (included under right-of-use assets) and
     buildings (included under property, plant and equipment) located         •   Obtaining an understanding from the Valuer about the
     in the People’s Republic of China (the “Land and Buildings”) are             valuation methodology, significant unobservable inputs and
     accounted for using revaluation model. As at 31 March 2022, the              data used in the valuations;
     carrying amount of the Group’s Land and Buildings at revaluation
     model are HK$328,600,000 and HK$229,817,000 respectively, which          •   Assessing the reasonableness of the price per square metre
     represented 10% and 7% of the Group’s total assets, respectively.            of comparable properties and information used by the Valuer
     The resulting revaluation loss of HK$36,170,000, representing sum            and management of the Group in the valuation model,
     of revaluation loss of HK$37,742,000 arising from right-of-use               including adjustment factors for locations and conditions of
     assets and revaluation gain of HK$1,572,000 arising from property,           the properties, in order to assess the appropriateness of these
     plant and equipment, respectively, has been charged to the other             inputs and comparing the comparable properties adopted with
     comprehensive income for the year ended 31 March 2022.                       fair market unit price; and

     As further disclosed in notes 4, 15 and 16 to the consolidated           •   Checking the mathematical accuracy of the valuation calculations.
     financial statements, in estimating the fair value of these Land and
     Buildings, the Group engaged an independent qualified valuer (the
     “Valuer”), to carry out the valuation. The management of the Group
     provided information to the Valuer and the Valuer established and
     determined the appropriate technique and inputs to the valuation
     model. The significant unobservable input of the approach is the
     price per square metre of comparable properties. Changes in the
     comparable properties adopted may result in a significant increase
     or decrease in fair value.

     由於在釐定重估價值時涉及固有的主觀判斷及複雜估計 ，我們將按                                           我們就管理層按重估模式對土地及樓宇估值進行的程序包括：
     重估模式估值使用權資產及物業 、廠房及設備識別為關鍵審計事
     項。                                                                       •   評估估值師的資格 、能力及客觀程度；

      貴集團位於中華人民共和國的租賃土地（計入使用權資產）及樓宇                                           •   向估值師了解估值方法 、重大不可觀察輸入數據及估值時
     （計入物業 、廠房及設備）    （「土地及樓宇」）乃採用重估模式入賬 。                                        所採用的數據；
      於二零二二年三月三十一日 ， 貴集團按重估模式估值的土地及
      樓宇的賬面值分別為328,600,000港元及229,817,000港元 ，分別                                •   評估估值師及 貴集團管理層於估值模式所用可資比較物
      佔 貴集團總資產的10%及7% 。分別來自使用權資產的重估虧損                                             業的每平方米價格及資料（包括就物業位置及狀況作出的調
      37,742,000港元及來自物業 、廠房及設備的重估收益1,572,000港                                     整因素）的合理性 ，以評估該等輸入數據的合適性 ，並按

     誠如綜合財務報表附註4 、15及16進一步披露 ，於估計該等土地及
     樓宇之公平值時 ， 貴集團已委聘合資格獨立估值師（「估值師」）進
     行估值 。 貴集團管理層向估值師提供資料 ，而估值師確立及釐
     定適用於估值模式的技術及輸入數據 。該方法之重大不可觀察輸入
     數據為可資比較物業的每平方米價格 。所採用可資比較物業的變動
     可導致公平值大幅上升或下降 。

2. APPLICATION OF AMENDMENTS TO HONG KONG FINANCIAL                                     2. 應用香港財務報告準則（「香港財務報告準則」）修
   REPORTING STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”)                                                          訂本
  Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily effective for the current                     本年度強制生效的香港財務報告準則修訂本
  year
  In the current year, the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as      為編製綜合財務報表 ，於本年度 ，本公司及其
  the “Group”) has applied the following amendments to HKFRSs issued by the               附屬公司（統稱為「本集團」）首次應用由香港會

       Impacts on application of the agenda decision of the Committee –                           應用委員會議程決定－銷售存貨所需成本（香港
       Costs necessary to sell inventories (HKAS 2 “Inventories” (“HKAS 2”))                      會計準則第2號「存貨」
                                                                                                            （「香港會計準則第2號」））
                                                                                                  的影響
       In June 2021, the Committee, through its agenda decision, clarified the costs              於二零二一年六月 ，委員會通過其議程決定 ，
       an entity should include as “estimated costs necessary to make the sale” when              釐清實體於釐定存貨的可變現淨值時應計入「成
       determining the net realisable value of inventories. In particular, whether such           功出售所需的估計成本」的成本 。具體而言 ，有
       costs should be limited to those that are incremental to the sale. The Committee           關成本是否應限於銷售增量成本 。委員會的結論
       concluded that the estimated costs necessary to make the sale should not be                為成功出售所需的估計成本不應限於增量成本 ，
       limited to those that are incremental but should also include costs that an entity         而亦應包括實體銷售其存貨必須產生的成本 ，包
       must incur to sell its inventories including those that are not incremental to a           括對特定銷售而言並非增量的成本 。
       particular sale.

       The Group’s accounting policy prior to the Committee’s agenda decision was                 於委員會的議程決定前 ，本集團的會計政策為於
       to determine the net realisable value of inventories taking into consideration             釐定存貨的可變現淨值時僅考慮增量成本 。應用
       incremental costs only. Upon application of the Committee’s agenda decision,               委員會的議程決定後 ，本集團將其會計政策變更
       the Group changed its accounting policy to determine the net realisable value              為於釐定存貨的可變現淨值時同時考慮增量成本
       of inventories taking into consideration both incremental costs and other costs            及銷售存貨所需的其他成本 。新會計政策已追溯
       necessary to sell inventories. The new accounting policy has been applied                  應用 。
       retrospectively.

       The application of the Committee’s agenda decision has had no material impact              應用委員會議程決定對本集團的財務狀況及表現
       on the Group’s financial positions and performance.                                        並無重大影響 。

3. BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                                3. 綜合財務報表編製基準及主要會計政策
   AND SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial statements                             3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準
       Statement of compliance                                                                   合規聲明
       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance                    綜合財務報表乃按照香港會計師公會頒佈之
       with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of the                   香港財務報告準則編製 。就編製綜合財務報
       consolidated financial statements, information is considered material if such             表而言 ，倘有資料合理預期將影響主要使用
       information is reasonably expected to influence decisions made by primary                 者所作出的決定 ，則該資料可被視作重大 。
       users. In addition, the consolidated financial statements include applicable              此外 ，綜合財務報表包括香港聯交所證券上
       disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The              市規則（「上市規則」）及香港公司條例規定之
       HK Stock Exchange (“Listing Rules”) and by the Hong Kong Companies                        適用披露 。
       Ordinance.

       Basis of preparation                                                                    編製基準
       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared under the                      除若干金融工具以及租賃土地及樓宇按下文
       historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments and leasehold            所闡釋會計政策以報告期末的公平值及重估
       land and buildings, which are measured at fair values and revalued amounts,             金額計量外 ，綜合財務報表按歷史成本基礎
       at the end of the reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies             編製 。
       set out below.

       Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the consideration given         歷史成本一般根據為交換貨品及服務而付出
       in exchange for goods and services.                                                     代價之公平值計算 。

       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to              公平值是於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序交
       transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants              易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付之價
       at the measurement date, regardless of whether that price is directly                   格 ，而不論該價格是否可直接觀察或使用其
       observable or estimated using another valuation technique. In estimating                他估值方法估計 。於估計資產或負債之公平
       the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the             值時 ，本集團會考慮市場參與者於計量日期
       characteristics of the asset or liability if the market participants would take         對資產或負債定價時所考慮的資產或負債特
       those characteristics into account when pricing the asset or liability at the           點 。綜合財務報表中作計量及╱或披露用途
       measurement date. Fair value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes                 之公平值乃按此基準釐定 ，惟於香港財務報
       in the consolidated financial statements is determined on such basis, except            告準則第2號「以股份為基礎付款」範圍內之
       for share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS                 以股份為基礎付款的交易 、於香港財務報告
       2 “Share-based Payment”, leasing transactions that are within the scope                 準則第16號「租賃」
                                                                                                        （「香港財務報告準則第16
       of HKFRS 16 “Leases” (“HKFRS 16”), and measurements that have some                      號」）範圍內之租賃交易及與公平值類似但並
       similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as net realisable value in      非公平值之計量（如香港會計準則第2號「存
       HKAS 2 “Inventories” or value in use in HKAS 36 “Impairment of Assets”.                 貨」之可變現淨值或香港會計準則第36號「資
                                                                                               產減值」之使用價值）除外 。

       A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account a                  非金融資產的公平值計量考慮了市場參與者
       market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using the asset           以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產或將該資產
       in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market participant that         出售予將以其最高及最佳用途使用該資產的
       would use the asset in its highest and best use.                                        另一市場參與者而產生經濟效益的能力 。

       In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value measurements are              此外 ，就財務報告而言 ，公平值計量根據公
       categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the degree to which the inputs                平值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及公平值計
       to the fair value measurements are observable and the significance of the               量之輸入數據對其整體重要性分類為第一 、
       inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety, which are described as            第二或第三級 ，詳情如下：
       follows:

       •   Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets                     •   第一級輸入數據為為實體於計量日期可
           for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the                   以取得的相同資產或負債在活躍市場之
           measurement date;                                                                       報價（未經調整）
                                                                                                          ；

       •   Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices included within                 •   第二級輸入數據為就資產或負債直接或
           Level 1, that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly or             間接可觀察之輸入數據（第一級內包括的
           indirectly; and                                                                         報價除外）
                                                                                                       ；及

       •   Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.                  •   第三級輸入數據為資產或負債的不可觀

       Revenue from contracts with customers                                                     來自客戶合約的收益
       The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance obligation                        本集團於完成履約責任之時（或就此）確認
       is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or services underlying the                 收益 ，即於特定履約責任相關貨品或服務的
       particular performance obligation is transferred to the customer.                        「控制權」移交客戶時 。

       A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a bundle of                   履約責任指特定的貨品或服務（或一組貨品
       goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods or services that      或服務）或一系列大致相同的特定貨品或服
       are substantially the same.                                                             務。

       Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over time by                 如符合以下其中一項標準 ，則控制權隨時間
       reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of the relevant                 轉移 ，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約責任
       performance obligation if one of the following criteria is met:                         的進展情況而隨時間確認：

       •   the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the benefits                      •   本集團履約時 ，客戶同時取得並耗用本
           provided by the Group’s performance as the Group performs;                              集團履約所提供的利益；

       •   the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that the                       •   本集團的履約產生或提升一項資產 ，而
           customer controls as the Group performs; or                                             該項資產於本集團履約時由客戶控制；
                                                                                                   或

       •   the Group’s performance does not create an asset with an alternative                •   本集團的履約並未產生令本集團有替代
           use to the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to payment                      用途的資產 ，且本集團對迄今已完成履
           for performance completed to date.                                                      約具有可強制執行的收取款項權利 。

       Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the customer                   否則 ，收益於客戶取得特定貨品或服務之控
       obtains control of the distinct good or service.                                        制權時確認 。

       A contract asset represents the Group’s right to consideration in exchange              合約資產指本集團就向客戶換取本集團已轉
       for goods or services that the Group has transferred to a customer that                 讓的貨品或服務收取代價的權利（尚未成為
       is not yet unconditional. It is assessed for impairment in accordance with              無條件）。其根據香港財務報告準則第9號「金
       HKFRS 9 “Financial Instruments” (“HKFRS 9”). In contrast, a receivable                  融工具」 （「香港財務報告準則第9號」）評估減
       represents the Group’s unconditional right to consideration, i.e. only the              值 。相反 ，應收款項指本集團收取代價的無
       passage of time is required before payment of that consideration is due.                條件權利 ，即只需待時間過去代價即須到期
                                                                                               支付 。

       A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer goods or             合約負債指本集團就已向客戶收取的代價（或
       services to a customer for which the Group has received consideration (or               代價到期的金額）而承擔向該客戶轉讓貨品
       an amount of consideration is due) from the customer.                                   或服務的責任 。

       A contract asset and a contract liability relating to the same contract are             有關同一合約之合約資產及合約負債按淨額
       accounted for and presented on a net basis.                                             基準入賬及呈列 。

       Leases                                                                                 租賃
       Definition of a lease                                                                  租賃的定義
       A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to               倘合約賦予在一段時期內控制一項已識別資
       control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in exchange for            產的使用權以換取代價 ，則該合約屬一項租
       consideration.                                                                         賃或包含一項租賃 。

       For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of initial                 對於首次應用香港財務報告準則第16號日期
       application of HKFRS 16 or arising from business combinations, the Group               或之後訂立或修訂或業務合併產生的合約 ，
       assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease based on the definition             本集團根據香港財務報告準則第16號項下的
       under HKFRS 16 at inception, modification date or acquisition date, as                 定義 ，於開始日 、修訂日或收購日（如適用）
       appropriate. Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and                 評估合約是否屬一項租賃或包含一項租賃 。
       conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.                                   除非合約條款及條件在後續發生變更 ，否則
                                                                                              不會對此類合約進行重新評估 。

       The Group as a lessee                                                                  本集團作為承租人
       Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                                對合約各組成部分的代價分配

       price of the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of the                組成部分的獨立價格合計 ，將合約的代價分
       non-lease components. Non-lease components are separated from lease                    配至各租賃組成部分 。非租賃組成部分與租
       component and are accounted for by applying other applicable standards.                賃組成部分分開 ，並採用其他適用準則入
                                                                                              賬。

           with the stand-alone price for the increase in scope and any appropriate              圍擴大對應的單獨價格 ，加上為反映特
           adjustments to that stand-alone price to reflect the circumstances of                 定合約的情況而對單獨價格進行的任何
           the particular contract.                                                              適當調整 。

       modified contract contains a lease component and one or more additional               合約包含租賃組成部分以及一個或多個額外

       price of the non-lease components.                                                    各租賃組成部分 。

             Government grants                                                                       政府補助
             Government grants are not recognised until there is reasonable assurance                與收入有關的政府補助直至可合理確保本集
             that the Group will comply with the conditions attaching to them and that               團將遵守補助所附帶的條件及收取補助時方
             the grants will be received.                                                            予以確認 。

             Government grants related to income that are receivable as compensation               與收入有關的政府補助乃作為已發生的開支
             for expenses or losses already incurred or for the purpose of giving                  或虧損補償的應收款項 、或是以給予本集團
             immediate financial support to the Group with no future related costs are             即時財務援助為目的而發放 ，無未來相關成
             recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they become receivable.           本 ，並在應收期間內於損益中確認 。該等補
             Such grants are presented under “other income”.                                       助乃於「其他收入」下呈列 。

             in which it is incurred.

                 benefits;                                                                             方式；

                 complete the development and to use or sell the intangible asset; and                 開發及使用或出售無形資產之可能性；

       Financial instruments                                                                     金融工具
       Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a group entity             倘集團實體成為工具合約條文的訂約方 ，則
       becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the instrument.                          金融資產及金融負債予以確認 。

       Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured at fair value         金融資產及金融負債最初按公平值計量 ，惟
       except for trade receivables arising from contracts with customers which                初步根據香港財務報告準則第15號計量之來
       are initially measured in accordance with HKFRS 15. Transaction costs                   自客戶合約所產生之貿易應收款項除外 。因
       that are directly attributable to the acquisition or issue of financial assets          收購或發行金融資產及金融負債而直接產生
       and financial liabilities are added to or deducted from the fair value of the           的交易成本於初始確認時於該項金融資產或
       financial assets or financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition.      金融負債的公平值中計入或扣除（如適用）。

       The effective interest method is a method of calculating the amortised cost             實際利率法乃計算金融資產或金融負債的攤
       of a financial asset or financial liability and of allocating interest income and       銷成本以及相關期間利息收入及利息開支分
       interest expense over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the           配的方法 。實際利率乃於初始確認時按金融
       rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments                 資產或金融負債的預計年期或適用的較短期
       (including all fees and points paid or received that form an integral part              間準確折現估計未來現金收入及付款（包括
       of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or                 構成實際利率不可或缺部分的全部已付或已
       discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or financial                收費用 、交易成本及其他溢價或折讓）至賬
       liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount          面淨值的利率 。
       on initial recognition.

       Financial assets                                                                        金融資產
       Classification and subsequent measurement of financial assets                           金融資產的分類及其後計量
       Financial assets that meet the following conditions are subsequently                    符合下列條件的金融資產其後按攤銷成本計
       measured at amortised cost:                                                             量：

       •   the financial asset is held within a business model whose objective is to           •   於目標為收取合約現金流量的業務模式
           collect contractual cash flows; and                                                     持有的金融資產；及

       •   the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to cash flows that               •   合約條款於指定日期產生現金流量 ，而
           are solely payments of principal and interest on the principal amount                   純粹作為本金及尚未清償本金的利息的
           outstanding.                                                                            付款 。

       All other financial assets are subsequently measured at fair value through               所有其他金融資產於其後按公平值計入損益
       profit or loss (“FVTPL”).                                                               （「按公平值計入損益」）計量 。

       (i) Amortised cost and interest income                                                  (i) 攤銷成本及利息收入
           Interest income is recognised using the effective interest method for                   其後按攤銷成本計量的金融資產的利息
           financial assets measured subsequently at amortised cost. Interest                      收入運用實際利率法確認 。利息收入透
           income is calculated by applying the effective interest rate to the gross               過應用實際利率於金融資產的總賬面值
           carrying amount of a financial asset, except for financial assets that have             計算，其後已信貸減值的金融資產除
           subsequently become credit-impaired. For financial assets that have                     外。就其後已信貸減值的金融資產而
           subsequently become credit-impaired, interest income is recognised by                   言，利息收入自下一報告期間起透過
           applying the effective interest rate to the amortised cost of the financial             應用實際利率於金融資產的攤銷成本確
           asset from the next reporting period. If the credit risk on the credit-                 認 。倘已信貸減值的金融工具的信貸風
           impaired financial instrument improves so that the financial asset is no                險有所改善 ，繼而令金融資產不再信貸
           longer credit-impaired, interest income is recognised by applying the                   減值 ，利息收入則自釐定該資產並無信
           effective interest rate to the gross carrying amount of the financial asset             貸減值後的報告期間初起透過應用實際
           from the beginning of the reporting period following the determination                  利率於金融資產的總賬面值確認 。
           that the asset is no longer credit-impaired.

       (i) Significant increase in credit risk                                                  (i) 信貸風險大幅增加
           In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly since                   於評估信貸風險自初始確認以來有否大
           initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a default occurring                  幅增加時 ，本集團比較金融工具於報告
           on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk                       日期出現違約的風險與該金融工具於初
           of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of                     始確認日期出現違約的風險 。作此評估
           initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group considers                      時 ，本集團會考慮合理並有理據支持的
           both quantitative and qualitative information that is reasonable and                     定量及定性資料 ，包括過往經驗及毋須
           supportable, including historical experience and forward-looking                         花費不必要成本或努力即可獲得的前瞻
           information that is available without undue cost or effort.                              性資料 。

           In particular, the following information is taken into account when                  尤其是 ，評估信貸風險有否大幅增加時
           assessing whether credit risk has increased significantly:                           會考慮下列資料：

           •    an actual or expected significant deterioration in the financial                •   金融工具外（如有）或內部信貸評級
                instrument’s external (if available) or internal credit rating;                     的實際或預期重大惡化；

                swap prices for the debtor;                                                         人的信貸違約掉期價大幅上升；

                decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations;                      行其債務責任的能力大幅下降；

                results of the debtor; and                                                          惡化；及

                obligations.

           presumes that the credit risk has increased significantly since initial              約付款逾期未付超過30天 ，則信貸風險

           demonstrates otherwise.                                                              並非如此 ，則作別論 。

            including the Group, in full.                                                          本集團）悉數付款 ，則發生違約事件 。

           Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default has occurred             儘管以上所述 ，本集團認為 ，倘金融資
           when a financial asset is more than 90 days past due unless the Group                產逾期超過90天時則發生違約 ，除非本
           has reasonable and supportable information to demonstrate that a                     集團有合理可靠的資料證明較寬鬆的違
           more lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                                  約標準屬更合適則當別論 。

             (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                                  (iii) 信貸減值的金融資產
                   A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events of default                 當發生一項或多項對金融資產估計未來
                   that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows of                    現金流量有不利影響的違約事件時 ，金
                   that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is                  融資產出現信貸減值 。金融資產信貸減
                   credit-impaired includes observable data about the following events:                    值的證據包括以下可觀察事件：

                 (a) significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;                 (a) 發行人或借款人出現重大財政困難；

                 (b) a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;                      (b) 違約 ，例如拖賬或逾期事項；

                 (c) the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual reasons              (c) 借款人的放款人因與借款人出現財
                     relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted to the              務困難有關之經濟或合約理由而給
                     borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                     予借款人在其他情況下放款人不予
                     consider; or                                                                        考慮之優惠條件；或

                 (d) it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter bankruptcy or              (d) 借款人有可能破產或進行其他財務
                     other financial reorganisation.                                                     重組 。

             (iv) Write-off policy                                                                 (iv) 撇銷政策
                  The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information                      倘有資料顯示交易對手方陷入嚴重財務
                  indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial difficulty and                困難且無實際可收回之期望（如交易對手
                  there is no realistic prospect of recovery, for example, when the                     方已進行清盤或進入破產程序 ，或如屬
                  counterparty has been placed under liquidation or has entered into                    貿易應收款項 ，該款項已逾期超過一年）
                  bankruptcy proceedings, or in the case of trade receivables, when the                （以較早發生者為準），本集團則撇銷金
                  amounts are over one year past due, whichever occurs sooner. Financial                融資產 。經考慮法律意見（如適用）後 ，
                  assets written off may still be subject to enforcement activities under               已撇銷之金融資產仍可能受制於本集團
                  the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account legal advice                     收回程序下之執法活動 。撇銷構成取消
                  where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition event. Any                 確認事件 。其後的任何收回均在損益確
                  subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.                               認。

             (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                                (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                 The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of default, loss              預期信貸虧損的計量依據為違約概率 、
                 given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and              違約損失率（即違約時的損失程度）及違
                 the exposure at default. The assessment of the probability of default                 約風險的函數 。評估違約概率及違約損
                 and loss given default is based on historical data and forward-looking                失率的依據是過往數據及前瞻性資料 。
                 information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                  預期信貸虧損的估計反映不偏不倚及概
                 weighted amount that is determined with the respective risks of default               率加權金額 ，以各自發生違約的風險為
                 occurring as the weights.                                                             權重釐定 。

                 Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual cash flows             一般而言 ，預期信貸虧損為根據合約應
                 that are due to the Group in accordance with the contract and the cash              付本集團的所有訂約現金流量與本集團
                 flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                預計收取的現金流量（按於初始確認時釐
                 interest rate determined at initial recognition.                                    定的實際利率折現）之間的差額 。

                 Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying amount of the             利息收入根據金融資產的總賬面值計
                 financial asset unless the financial asset is credit-impaired, in which case        算，惟金融資產為信貸減值的情況除
                 interest income is calculated based on amortised cost of the financial              外 ，於此情況下 ，利息收入根據金融資
                 asset.                                                                              產的攤銷成本計算 。

                 The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in profit or loss for               本集團透過調整所有金融工具的賬面值
                 all financial instruments by adjusting their carrying amount, with the              於損益確認其減值收益或虧損 ，惟貿易
                 exception of trade receivables, trade receivables from fellow subsidiaries          應收款項 、來自同系附屬公司的貿易應
                 and contract assets where the corresponding adjustment is recognised                收款項及合約資產除外 ，其相應調整乃
                 through a loss allowance account.                                                   透過虧損撥備賬確認 。

       An equity instrument is any contract that evidences a residual interest in              權益工具乃證明實體的資產於扣除所有負債

       bank borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised cost, using the                  在內的金融負債其後使用實際利率法按攤銷
       effective interest method.                                                              成本計量 。

       Interest expense is recognised on an effective interest basis.                          利息開支按實際利率法確認 。

       Derecognition                                                                           取消確認
       The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the contractual rights               本集團僅於資產收取現金流量的合約權利屆
       to the cash flows from the asset expire, or when it transfers the financial             滿或轉移金融資產及其所有權的絕大部分風
       asset and substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership of the asset             險及回報予其他實體時取消確認金融資產 。
       to another entity.

       On derecognition of a financial asset at amortised cost, the difference                 於取消確認按攤銷成本計量的金融資產時 ，
       between the asset’s carrying amount and the sum of the consideration                    資產賬面值與已收及應收代價的總和間的差
       received and receivable is recognised in profit or loss.                                額 ，會於損益確認 。

       The Group derecognises financial liabilities when, and only when, the                   本集團只有在其責任獲免除 、取消或屆滿
       Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or have expired. The difference           時 ，方會取消確認金融負債 。獲取消確認的
       between the carrying amount of the financial liability derecognised and the             金融負債的賬面值與已付及應付代價間的差
       consideration paid and payable is recognised in profit or loss.                         額於損益確認 。

       the practical expedient to account for these changes by updating the                    法 ，將該等變動透過更新實際利率入賬 ，有
       effective interest rate, such change in effective interest rate normally has            關實際利率變動一般並不會對相關金融資產
       no significant effect on the carrying amount of the relevant financial asset            或金融負債之賬面值造成重大影響 。
       or financial liability.

           the change).

       by interest rate benchmark reform, the Group first applies the practical                 礎出現的變動外），本集團首先對利率基準改

             hedging instrument is effective in offsetting changes in fair values or cash        是否有效抵銷因對沖風險而引致對沖項目之

             the hedging relationships meet all of the following hedge effectiveness             下列所有對沖成效規定時：
             requirements:

                 hedging instrument;

             meets the qualifying criteria again.                                                格標準 。

4. KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY AND CRITICAL                                   4. 估計不明朗因素之主要來源及關鍵會計判斷
   ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENT
   In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are described            於應用本集團之會計政策（於附註3陳述）時 ，本
   in note 3, the directors of the Company are required to make judgements,              公司董事需對未能透過其他來源直接確定的資產
   estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities        及負債之賬面值作出判斷 、估計及假設 。該等
   that are not readily apparent from other sources. The estimates and associated        估計及相關假設乃基於過往經驗及其他被視為相
   assumptions are based on historical experience and other factors that are             關之因素 。實際結果可能有別於該等估計 。
   considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.

   The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-going basis.           估計及相關假設乃按持續基準被審閱 。如對會計
   Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in the period in which the           估計之修訂僅影響進行修訂之期間 ，則於修訂估
   estimate is revised if the revision affects only that period, or in the period of     計的期間確認修訂 ，或如修訂同時影響目前及未
   the revision and future periods if the revision affects both current and future       來期間 ，則於修訂及未來期間確認 。
   periods.

   Critical judgement in applying accounting policies                                    應用會計政策的關鍵判斷
   The following is the critical judgement, apart from those involving estimations       以下為董事於應用本集團會計政策過程中作出的
   (see below), that the directors have made in the process of applying the Group’s      關鍵判斷（涉及估計者（見下文）除外），該等判
   accounting policies and that have the most significant effect on the amounts          斷對綜合財務報表內確認的金額造成非常重大影
   recognised in the consolidated financial statements.                                  響。

   Deferred tax in respect of temporary differences attributable to the                  就中華人民共和國（「中國」）附屬公司未分派溢
   undistributed profits of subsidiaries in the People’s Republic of China               利應佔暫時差額的遞延稅項
   (the “PRC”)

         to allocate resources to segments and to assess their performance. Specifically,       評估其表現而定期審閱有關本集團組成部分的內
         the Group’s reportable segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into two main              部報告識別 。具體而言 ，本集團根據香港財務
         operating divisions – (i) cable assembly and (ii) digital cable (previously named      報告準則第8號的可呈報分部分為兩大經營分部–
         as “networking cables”).                                                               (i)電線組件及(ii)數字電線（先前稱為「網絡電線」）
                                                                                                                           。

30. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES                                                          30. 退休福利計劃
   The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme for all qualifying                 本集團為香港的所有合資格僱員參與強制性公積

   contributes 5% of the relevant payroll costs to the scheme, subject to a cap            向該計劃支付相關入息的5%作為供款 ，而上限
   of monthly relevant income of HK$30,000 of employees, which contribution                為僱員每月有關入息30,000港元 ，有關供款乃與
   is matched by the employees. The Group also makes voluntary contribution in             僱員的供款一致 。除強制性供款外 ，本集團亦
   addition to the mandatory contribution.                                                 作出自願供款 。

   The subsidiary is required to contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs to     按工資成本的指定百分比向退休福利計劃供款以
   the retirement benefits scheme to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the         為有關福利撥資 。本集團在退休福利計劃下的唯
   Group with respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make the specified           一責任為作出指定供款 。
   contributions.

31. MAJOR NON-CASH TRANSACTION                                                           31. 主要非現金交易
   On 24 March 2020, the Company entered into a sale and purchase agreement                於二零二零年三月二十四日 ，本公司與本公司
   (“Sale and Purchase Agreement”) with Linkz Industries, the then ultimate                當時的最終控股公司領先工業訂立買賣協議（「買
   holding company of the Company, pursuant to which the Company has                       賣協議」），據此 ，本公司有條件同意收購及領先
   conditionally agreed to acquire, and Linkz Industries has conditionally agreed to       工業有條件同意出售華迅電纜有限公司（「目標公
   sell entire issued share capital of Linkz Cables Limited (the “Target Company”,         司」，連同其於完成集團重組（「華迅電纜重組」）
   together with its subsidiaries upon completion of a group reorganisation (the           後的附屬公司統稱為「目標集團」）全部已發行股
   “Linkz Cables Reorganisation”), collectively referred to as the “Target Group”),        本及於完成日期目標集團結欠領先工業的股東貸
   and the shareholder’s loan owed by the Target Group to Linkz Industries at              款（「收購事項」）。收購事項的代價為完成資產淨
   completion date (the “Acquisition”). The consideration of the Acquisition               值的95% ，界定為(i)目標集團於完成日期的綜
   is 95% of the completion net asset value, being defined as the sum of (i)               合資產淨值；(ii)目標集團的土地及樓宇公平值收
   consolidated net asset value of the Target Group at the date of completion;             益 ，即於完成日期的土地及樓宇賬面值與公平值
   (ii) the fair value gain of land and buildings of the Target Group, being the           之差額；及(iii)目標集團於完成日期所產生或結欠
   difference between the book value and the fair value of the land and buildings          領先工業的所有責任及負債的貨幣價值（「股東貸
   at the date of completion; and (iii) the monetary value of all obligations and          款」）之總和 。
   liabilities incurred or owing by the Target Group to Linkz Industries at the date
   of completion (the “Shareholders’ Loan”).

   The Linkz Cables Reorganisation and the Acquisition were completed on                   華迅電纜重組及收購事項於二零二零年六月三十
   30 June 2020 (“Completion Date”). The consideration was amounted to                     日（「完成日期」）完成 。代價為數780,993,000港

   and settlement of the Shareholders’ Loan of HK$122,237,000. The consideration           東貸款122,237,000港元 。代價由本公司透過現
   was satisfied by the Company through the payment in cash.                               金付款結付 。

   of HK$658,756,000 paid for acquisition of equity interests and the share capital        658,756,000港元與目標公司股本8,390,000港元

36. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                                    36. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will be able               為確保本集團的實體將可持續經營 ，本集團對其
   to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to stakeholders                    資本進行管理 ，並透過使債務及權益達致最佳平
   through the optimisation of the debt and equity balances. The Group’s overall                 衡為持份者締造最大回報 。本集團的整體策略於
   strategy remains unchanged throughout the year.                                               整個年度內維持不變 。

   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debts, which includes                      本集團的資本架構包括債務淨額（分別包括附註
   unsecured bank borrowings and lease liabilities in notes 25 and 24 respectively,              25及24的無抵押銀行借款及租賃負債），扣除現
   net of cash and cash equivalents and equity attributable to owners of the                     金及現金等價物以及本公司擁有人應佔權益（包
   Company, comprising issued share capital and reserves.                                        括已發行資本及儲備）。

   The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a regular basis.                 本公司董事定期審閱資本架構 。作為該審閱的一
   As part of this review, the directors of the Company consider the cost of capital             部分 ，本公司董事考慮資本成本及與資本相關的
   and the risks associated with the capital. Based on recommendations of the                    風險 。根據本公司董事的建議 ，本集團將透過
   directors of the Company, the Group will balance its overall capital structure                派付股息 、發行新股份 、籌措或償還銀行借款
   through the payment of dividends, new share issues and raising or repayment                   平衡其整體資本架構 。
   of bank borrowings.

39. EVENT AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                                                           39. 報告期後事項
   On 19 April 2022, Luxshare, the ultimate holding company of the Company,                          於二零二二年四月十九日 ，本公司最終控股公司
   acquired and cancelled of 45,296,000 outstanding share options of the Group.                      立訊收購及註銷本公司未行使的45,296,000份購
   The Group would account for the cancellation as an acceleration of vesting and                    股權 。本集團將取消的購股權記入加速歸屬 ，
   recognise immediately the amount that otherwise would have been recognised                        除歸屬期剩餘時間內接受的服務以外 ，立即確認
   for services received over the remainder of the vesting period. Details of the                    加速歸屬的金額 。交易的詳情載於本公司日期為
   transaction are set out in the Company’s announcement dated 19 April 2022.                        二零二二年四月十九日的公告內 。
